# Carcassonne

> Chinese name: **卡卡颂 Carcassonne** · Doc #102 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../102_%E5%8D%A1%E5%8D%A1%E9%A2%82_Carcassonne.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/102)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–5 (best: 2-3) |
| Play time | 30–45 min |
| Age | 7+ |
| Complexity | ●●○○○ Light (2/5) |
| Tags | tile placement, area control, family, classic |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** E4 / F2 / F3
- **Photos:** [155102](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155102.jpg), [155114](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155114.jpg), [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg), [204000](../../photos/web/IMG20261127204000.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 第三轮照片 155102：E4 层 → 卡卡颂 Carcassonne
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155114 / 155121：F2 / F3 行 → ★ 现代艺术、★ 山屋惊魂、★ 出包魔法师、★ 小小城镇、★ 只言片语 ×2、★ 卡卡颂 ×2+、★ 拉斯维加斯 豪华版 + 普通版
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 204000：上层左侧（铁网后货架） → 最上面 蓝色 Carcassonne 盒

## Overview

Draw and place tiles to build medieval France; place meeples as robbers, knights, monks or farmers to score features.

## Setup

1. Place the start tile; each player takes meeples.

## How to play

1. Draw a tile and place it matching edges.
2. Optionally place a meeple on a feature of that tile (not one already occupied).
3. Completed features score immediately; return meeples.

## Game end & scoring

- When tiles run out, score incomplete features and farms; most points wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** When tiles run out, score incomplete features and farms; most points wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Draw a tile and place it matching edges.
3. **Setup check:** Place the start tile; each player takes meeples.
4. **The one rule people forget:** You cannot place on an occupied connected feature.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Meeples are limited.
- Farmers decide close games.
- Join features to contest majorities.

## Common mistakes

- You cannot place on an occupied connected feature.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** most board gamers
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

> Written by the editor from the rules above as a walkthrough; numbers in the example are illustrative. The rulebook in the box always wins.

### Component checklist

From the Chinese doc (original wording):

- [ ] 72 块地形板块（含起始板块，背面不同；第二版另附河流、修道院长小扩展）、每人 7 个米宝（第二版加 1 个修道院长）、计分版图。

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. Draw a tile and place it matching edges.
2. Place a meeple on the city.
3. The city completes: 2 tiles × 2 = 4 points; meeple returns.
4. His road connects to mine, sharing.
5. Score incomplete features and farms at the end.

### Scoring example

- City 2/tile (+2 shield), road 1/tile, cloister 9.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- **Q: Can I place on an occupied city?**  
  A: No, but you can connect.

### Player-count differences

- 2–5.
- Best with: 2-3 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- Many expansions.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 10 min (weight ●●).
- Play: 30–45 min; budget about 55 min for a first game.
- Total: about 65 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Draw a tile and place it matching edges. Optionally place a meeple as a thief, knight, monk or farmer. Completed roads, cities and monasteries score immediately and return meeples. Cities 2 per tile (+2 per shield), roads 1 per tile, monastery 9. Endgame scores incomplete features and farms.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Keep meeples in reserve.
- **Mid:** Farmers on big fields score big.
- **Late:** Place last meeples on near-complete features.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Can I join an occupied city directly?**  
  A: No, but connections can merge.
- **Q: Tied majority?**  
  A: All tied players score fully.

---

<a id="g103"></a>

# Sheriff of Nottingham (2nd Ed.)

> Chinese name: **诺丁汉警长第二版** · Doc #103 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../103_%E8%AF%BA%E4%B8%81%E6%B1%89%E8%AD%A6%E9%95%BF%E7%AC%AC%E4%BA%8C%E7%89%88_Sheriff_of_Nottingham.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/103)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 3–6 (best: 4-5) |
| Play time | 60 min |
| Age | 14+ |
| Complexity | ●●○○○ Light (2/5) |
| Tags | bluffing, bribery, negotiation, talky |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** D4 / D5
- **Photos:** [155102](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155102.jpg), [155106](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155106.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 第三轮照片 155102 / 155106：D4、D5 层 → 诺丁汉警长 第二版 Sheriff of Nottingham

## Overview

Smuggle goods into the city: bag goods, declare a legal type and count; the sheriff inspects or lets you pass — bribes welcome.

## Setup

1. Deal goods; rotate sheriff.

## How to play

1. Market: exchange cards.
2. Load up to 5 cards and declare one legal type with the true count.
3. The sheriff inspects or passes; lies are fined; honest merchants are compensated.

## Game end & scoring

- After everyone has been sheriff (set times), score goods, coins, king/queen bonuses.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** After everyone has been sheriff (set times), score goods, coins, king/queen bonuses.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Market: exchange cards.
3. **Setup check:** Deal goods; rotate sheriff.
4. **The one rule people forget:** The count must be true; the type may be a lie.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Be honest sometimes.
- Bribe with goods and promises.
- Legal majorities matter.

## Common mistakes

- The count must be true; the type may be a lie.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** talkative groups who enjoy acting and bluffing
- **Not ideal for:** players who dislike conflict or backstabbing

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

> Written by the editor from the rules above as a walkthrough; numbers in the example are illustrative. The rulebook in the box always wins.

### Component checklist

From the Chinese doc (original wording):

- [ ] 货物卡：**合法货物**（苹果、奶酪、面包、鸡）、**违禁品**（胡椒、蜂蜜酒、丝绸、弩等）、皇家货物、商人袋子（带扣）、金币、商人摊位板、警长标记。

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. A is sheriff.
2. Others bag goods and declare.
3. A inspects my bag: honest — he pays me.
4. B's bag isn't inspected; contraband gets through.
5. Score goods and king/queen bonuses.

### Scoring example

- Goods + contraband + coins + bonuses.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- **Q: Can bribes be promises?**  
  A: Yes, but non-binding.

### Player-count differences

- 3–6.
- Best with: 4-5 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- See rulebook.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 10 min (weight ●●).
- Play: 60–60 min; budget about 70 min for a first game.
- Total: about 80 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Smuggle contraband into the city. Bag goods and declare (truth or lie); the Sheriff inspects or lets it pass. Caught? Pay fines. Wrong? Sheriff pays you. Bribes and deals allowed. Score goods, contraband, coins and king/queen bonuses.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Be honest early to build trust.
- **Mid:** As sheriff, note who lies.
- **Late:** Smuggle big late.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Can a bribe be a promise?**  
  A: Yes, not enforced.
- **Q: Max cards in bag?**  
  A: 5.

---

<a id="g104"></a>

# King of Tokyo: Dark Edition

> Chinese name: **东京之王 暗黑无界** · Doc #104 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../104_%E4%B8%9C%E4%BA%AC%E4%B9%8B%E7%8E%8B%E6%9A%97%E9%BB%91%E6%97%A0%E7%95%8C_King_of_Tokyo_Dark_Edition.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/104)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–6 (best: 4-5) |
| Play time | 30 min |
| Age | 8+ |
| Complexity | ●○○○○ Very light (1/5) |
| Tags | dice, push-your-luck, brawl, family |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** D4 / D5
- **Photos:** [155102](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155102.jpg), [155106](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155106.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 第三轮照片 155102 / 155106：D4、D5 层 → 东京之王：暗黑无界 King of Tokyo: Dark Edition

## Overview

Monster brawl: roll Yahtzee-style dice to score, heal, gain energy or attack; holding Tokyo scores but takes hits.

## Setup

1. Choose monsters (10 health, 0 VP); reveal 3 power cards.

## How to play

1. Roll 6 dice up to 3 times.
2. Resolve: triples of numbers score; energy; claws attack (Tokyo vs outside); hearts heal (not in Tokyo).
3. Buy cards with energy; wickedness track (Dark Edition).

## Game end & scoring

- First to 20 VP or last monster standing wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** First to 20 VP or last monster standing wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Roll 6 dice up to 3 times.
3. **Setup check:** Choose monsters (10 health, 0 VP); reveal 3 power cards.
4. **The one rule people forget:** You may leave Tokyo only when attacked.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Leave Tokyo when low.
- Energy cards give long-term power.
- Switch between VP and survival.

## Common mistakes

- You may leave Tokyo only when attacked.
- +2 VP for starting your turn in Tokyo.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** icebreakers; newcomers and grandparents can learn it instantly
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

1. Roll 6 dice, reroll twice.
2. Three 3s: 3 points; 2 claws deal damage.
3. The monster in Tokyo yields; I enter (+1).
4. Start my turn in Tokyo: +2.
5. First to 20 wins.

- 20 VP or last survivor.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- Play: 30–30 min; budget about 35 min for a first game.
- Total: about 40 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Monsters brawl. Roll 6 dice with 2 rerolls: points, heal, energy, attack. Tokyo hits everyone outside; outsiders hit Tokyo. Staying in Tokyo scores but you can't heal with dice. First to 20 or last alive wins.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Avoid Tokyo early; build energy.
- **Mid:** Enter Tokyo with high HP.
- **Late:** Race to 20 or eliminate.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Heal in Tokyo?**  
  A: Not with dice.
- **Q: Leave Tokyo when hit?**  
  A: Yes, attacker enters.

<a id="g105"></a>

# Catan

> Chinese name: **卡坦岛 Catan** · Doc #105 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../105_%E5%8D%A1%E5%9D%A6%E5%B2%9B_%E4%B8%80%E7%99%BE%E6%B8%B8%E7%89%88_Catan.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/105)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 3–4 (best: 4) |
| Play time | 60–90 min |
| Age | 10+ |
| Complexity | ●●○○○ Light (2/5) |
| Tags | trading, dice rolling, route building, classic, family |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** D4 / D5
- **Photos:** [155102](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155102.jpg), [155106](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155106.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 第三轮照片 155102 / 155106：D4、D5 层 → 一百游 卡坦岛 KATAN ISLAND

## Overview

Roll for wood, brick, sheep, wheat and ore; build roads, settlements and cities; first to 10 points.

## Setup

1. Build the map (6 and 8 not adjacent); snake-place two settlements and roads.

## How to play

1. Roll for production.
2. Trade with players or the bank (4:1, ports).
3. Build roads, settlements, cities, development cards.
4. On a 7, discard half if over 7 cards and move the robber.

## Game end & scoring

- First to 10 VP on their turn wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** First to 10 VP on their turn wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Roll for production.
3. **Setup check:** Build the map (6 and 8 not adjacent); snake-place two settlements and roads.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Settlements must be 2 edges apart.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Opening spots: probability and diversity.
- Ports convert surplus.
- Do not trade with the leader.

## Common mistakes

- Settlements must be 2 edges apart.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** most board gamers
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

> Written by the editor from the rules above as a walkthrough; numbers in the example are illustrative. The rulebook in the box always wins.

### Component checklist

From the Chinese doc (original wording):

- [ ] 19 块地形板块（森林 4 → 木材、牧场 4 → 羊毛、田地 4 → 小麦、丘陵 3 → 砖块、山脉 3 → 矿石、沙漠 1）、数字圆片、海框与港口、资源卡、发展卡 25 张、每人 15 条道路 / 5 个村庄 / 4 座城市、强盗、“最长道路”和“最大骑士团”卡、2 颗骰子。

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. 2 settlements and 2 roads each.
2. Roll 8: my settlement gains wheat.
3. I build a road for wood and brick.
4. A 7: discard over 7 cards; move the robber.
5. First to 10 points wins.

### Scoring example

- Settlements 1, cities 2, longest road/largest army 2; first to 10.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- **Q: Bank trades?**  
  A: 4:1, ports 3:1 or 2:1.

### Player-count differences

- 3–4.
- Best with: 4 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- Expansions.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 10 min (weight ●●).
- Play: 60–90 min; budget about 110 min for a first game.
- Total: about 120 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Rolls produce wood, brick, sheep, wheat, ore. Build roads, settlements, cities, buy development cards. Trade with players, bank 4:1, ports 3:1/2:1. A 7 moves the robber. Settlement 1, city 2, longest road/largest army 2 each; first to 10 wins.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Pick diverse numbers; 6 and 8 are best.
- **Mid:** Trade for what you lack.
- **Late:** Grab longest road or largest army.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Trade with the bank?**  
  A: 4:1, ports 3:1 or 2:1.
- **Q: Over 7 cards on a 7?**  
  A: Discard half.

# Dungeon Fighter

> Chinese name: **地城勇士 Dungeon Fighter** · Doc #106 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../106_%E5%9C%B0%E5%9F%8E%E5%8B%87%E5%A3%AB_Dungeon_Fighter.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/106)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 1–6 (best: 4) |
| Play time | 45 min |
| Age | 8+ |
| Complexity | ●○○○○ Very light (1/5) |
| Tags | co-op, flicking, dice, party |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** D4 / D5
- **Photos:** [155102](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155102.jpg), [155106](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155106.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 第三轮照片 155102 / 155106：D4、D5 层 → 地城勇士 Dungeon Fighter

## Overview

Cooperative dungeon crawl with dexterity: bounce dice (behind your back, eyes closed…) onto a target to hit monsters.

## Setup

1. Place the target board; heroes with health; shared resources.

## How to play

1. Throw dice at the target per the monster's throwing rule.
2. Damage depends on the ring hit.
3. Shop for items between rooms.

## Game end & scoring

- Defeat the final boss to win; lose if all heroes fall.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Defeat the final boss to win; lose if all heroes fall.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Throw dice at the target per the monster's throwing rule.
3. **Setup check:** Place the target board; heroes with health; shared resources.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Throws off the target miss.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Practise a steady bounce.
- Save items for hard monsters.
- Let your best thrower lead.

## Common mistakes

- Throws off the target miss.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** friends who prefer working together; icebreakers; newcomers and grandparents can learn it instantly
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

1. Choose a hero to attack a monster.
2. Bounce dice onto a target with special throws.
3. Hits deal damage.
4. The monster strikes back.
5. Defeat the boss together.

### Scoring example

- Beat the boss; lose if heroes die.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- See “Common mistakes” above; anything else — check the rulebook.

### Player-count differences

- 1–6.
- Best with: 4 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- Expansions.

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

- Play: 45–45 min; budget about 55 min for a first game.
- Total: about 60 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Practise your throws.
- **Mid:** Use skills and items.
- **Late:** Focus the boss.

# Las Vegas Royale

> Chinese name: **拉斯维加斯 Royale** · Doc #107 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../107_%E6%8B%89%E6%96%AF%E7%BB%B4%E5%8A%A0%E6%96%AF%E7%9A%87%E5%AE%B6_Las_Vegas_Royale.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/107)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–5 (best: 4-5) |
| Play time | 30–45 min |
| Age | 8+ |
| Complexity | ●○○○○ Very light (1/5) |
| Tags | dice, majority, family, push-your-luck |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** D4 / D5 / E5 / F2 / F3
- **Photos:** [155102](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155102.jpg), [155106](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155106.jpg), [155114](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155114.jpg), [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 第三轮照片 155102 / 155106：D4、D5 层 → 拉斯维加斯 皇家版 Las Vegas Royale（+ E5 的 1 盒 alea 版）
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155114 / 155121：F2 / F3 行 → ★ 现代艺术、★ 山屋惊魂、★ 出包魔法师、★ 小小城镇、★ 只言片语 ×2、★ 卡卡颂 ×2+、★ 拉斯维加斯 豪华版 + 普通版

## Overview

Roll 8 dice and place all of one number in that casino; most dice takes the top bill, but tied players cancel out.

## Setup

1. 6 casinos with money; each player has 8 dice (plus neutral dice in small games).

## How to play

1. Roll your remaining dice; choose one number and place all those dice on that casino.
2. When all dice are placed, pay out: highest gets the biggest bill; tied players get nothing.

## Game end & scoring

- After 4 rounds, most money wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** After 4 rounds, most money wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Roll your remaining dice; choose one number and place all those dice on that casino.
3. **Setup check:** 6 casinos with money; each player has 8 dice (plus neutral dice in small games).
4. **The one rule people forget:** Ties cancel and the next player down collects.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Create ties to cancel opponents.
- Late placers have more information.
- Small casinos can be cheap wins.

## Common mistakes

- Ties cancel and the next player down collects.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** icebreakers; newcomers and grandparents can learn it instantly
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

- [ ] No complete public component list — use the list in the box.

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. Each casino gets at least $50,000.
2. I roll 8 dice and place all 4s on casino 4.
3. A ties me there.
4. Ties cancel; the next player takes the top bill.
5. Most cash after 4 rounds.

### Scoring example

- Most cash after 4 rounds.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- **Q: Ties?**  
  A: All tied players get nothing.

### Player-count differences

- 2–5.
- Best with: 4-5 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- Royale adds modules.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 5 min (weight ●).
- Play: 30–45 min; budget about 55 min for a first game.
- Total: about 60 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Roll 8 dice and place all of one number in the matching casino. When all dice are placed, the player with most dice in a casino takes the biggest bill; ties cancel out. Most money after four rounds wins.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Place in high-value casinos.
- **Mid:** Create ties to cancel opponents.
- **Late:** Use last dice for key spots.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Must I place all dice of a number?**  
  A: Yes.
- **Q: Ties?**  
  A: They cancel out.

# Sleeping Queens

> Chinese name: **睡皇后 Sleeping Queens** · Doc #108 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../108_%E7%9D%A1%E7%9A%87%E5%90%8E_Sleeping_Queens.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/108)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–5 (best: 3-4) |
| Play time | 20 min |
| Age | 8+ |
| Complexity | ●○○○○ Very light (1/5) |
| Tags | kids, family, cards, arithmetic |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** B4 / C3 / C4
- **Photos:** [155056](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155056.jpg), [204000](../../photos/web/IMG20261127204000.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 第三轮照片 155056：C3 / C4 层、B4 左下角、右侧 → 睡皇后 Sleeping Queens
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 204000：上层中部（铁网后货架） → 左侧 橙色小盒一摞

## Overview

Wake the sleeping queens: kings wake, knights steal, potions put to sleep; arithmetic plays discard many cards.

## Setup

1. 12 queens face down in the centre; deal 5 cards.

## How to play

1. Play a king to wake a queen, a knight to steal (dragon blocks), a potion to put one to sleep (wand blocks).
2. Or discard a number, a pair, or an addition equation, then draw.

## Game end & scoring

- Win with 5 queens or 50 points (4–5 players: 4/40).

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Win with 5 queens or 50 points (4–5 players: 4/40).
2. **What you do on a turn:** Play a king to wake a queen, a knight to steal (dragon blocks), a potion to put one to sleep (wand blocks).
3. **Setup check:** 12 queens face down in the centre; deal 5 cards.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Cat and Dog Queens cannot be held together.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Equations cycle bad cards.
- Keep a dragon or wand.
- The Rose Queen wakes another.

## Common mistakes

- Cat and Dog Queens cannot be held together.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** families with young children; short gaps — waiting for friends, before or after dinner
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

> Written by the editor from the rules above as a walkthrough; numbers in the example are illustrative. The rulebook in the box always wins.

### Component checklist

From the Chinese doc (original wording):

- [ ] 12 张皇后**（每张 5–20 分，如玫瑰皇后、猫皇后、狗皇后、松饼皇后等）
- [ ] 8 张国王**（叫醒皇后）
- [ ] 4 张骑士**（偷别人的皇后）
- [ ] 3 张龙**（挡骑士）
- [ ] 4 张安眠药水**（让别人的皇后睡回去）
- [ ] 3 张魔杖**（挡药水）
- [ ] 5 张小丑**（翻牌碰运气）
- [ ] 数字牌 1–10**（各 4 张）

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. 12 queens face down.
2. I play a King to wake a queen.
3. He plays a Knight; I block with a Dragon.
4. I discard an equation 2+3=5.
5. I reach the queen/points goal.

### Scoring example

- 2–3 players: 5 queens or 50 points; 4–5 players: 4 queens or 40 points; if all queens are awake, highest points wins.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- See “Common mistakes” above; anything else — check the rulebook.

### Player-count differences

- 2–5.
- Best with: 3-4 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- See rulebook.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 5 min (weight ●).
- Play: 20–20 min; budget about 25 min for a first game.
- Total: about 30 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Wake the Sleeping Queens. Kings wake a queen; Knights steal (Dragon blocks); Potions put queens back to sleep (Wand blocks); Jesters flip to decide; number equations let you discard several cards. 2–3 players: 5 queens or 50 points; 4–5: 4 queens or 40.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Wake high-value queens first.
- **Mid:** Keep Dragon and Wand for defence.
- **Late:** Steal the last queens.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Rose Queen?**  
  A: Wake another queen with her.
- **Q: Cat and Dog queens together?**  
  A: No.

## Overview

Classic icebreaker: the chosen player picks truth or dare.

## Setup

## How to play

## Game end & scoring

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** No winner — play until the mood changes.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Selected player chooses truth or dare and draws a card.
3. **Setup check:** Choose a selection method.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Respect everyone's limits.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Filter cards for the group.

## Common mistakes

- Respect everyone's limits.

## Good for / not for

# Cubeez

> Chinese name: **变脸魔方 Cubeez** · Doc #110 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../110_%E5%8F%98%E8%84%B8%E9%AD%94%E6%96%B9_Cubeez.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/110)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–4 (best: 2-4) |
| Play time | 15 min |
| Age | 6+ |
| Complexity | ●○○○○ Very light (1/5) |
| Tags | real-time, spatial, reaction, kids, family |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** B4 / C3 / C4
- **Photos:** [155056](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155056.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 第三轮照片 155056：C3 / C4 层、B4 左下角、右侧 → 变脸魔方 Cubeez（铁盒）

## Overview

Blue Orange real-time puzzle: rotate, flip and swap your 3 wooden cubes to build the funny face shown on a challenge card.

## Setup

1. 12 wooden cubes (4 colours × 3, each face printed with eyes, mouths etc.) and 50 challenge cards.
2. Each player takes 3 cubes of one colour; the youngest starts.

## How to play

1. Reveal a challenge card showing a 3-cube face.
2. Everyone simultaneously turns, flips and swaps their cubes to match.
3. The first to finish puts a hand on the card; if correct they win it.
4. Reveal the next card.

## Game end & scoring

- Play to the target number of cards in the rulebook; most cards wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Play to the target number of cards in the rulebook; most cards wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Reveal a challenge card showing a 3-cube face.
3. **Setup check:** 12 wooden cubes (4 colours × 3, each face printed with eyes, mouths etc.) and 50 challenge cards.
4. **The one rule people forget:** A wrong build loses the card (per rulebook).
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Find the eyes first.
- Learn which features share a cube.
- Orientation matters.

## Common mistakes

- A wrong build loses the card (per rulebook).

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** families with young children; short gaps — waiting for friends, before or after dinner; fans of speed and excitement
- **Not ideal for:** people who want to think slowly and quietly

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

> Written by the editor from the rules above as a walkthrough; numbers in the example are illustrative. The rulebook in the box always wins.

### Component checklist

From the Chinese doc (original wording):

- [ ] **12 个木制方块**（4 种颜色，每色 3 个；每面印着眼睛、嘴巴等表情局部）、**50 张挑战卡**（6×6 cm）、多语言说明书（含简体中文）

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. Three cubes each.
2. Flip a challenge card.
3. Everyone races to match the face.
4. I finish first, slap the card and score.
5. First to 10 cards wins.

### Scoring example

- First to 10 cards.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- **Q: If wrong?**  
  A: Out for that round.

### Player-count differences

- 2–4.
- Best with: 2-4 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- 3D cards require stacking.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 5 min (weight ●).
- Play: 15–15 min; budget about 20 min for a first game.
- Total: about 25 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Rotate 3 wooden cubes to match a monster face on the card — fastest wins the card.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Learn each cube's faces.
- **Mid:** Start with the easiest feature.
- **Late:** Stay calm.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Wrong match?**  
  A: Penalty per rulebook.
- **Q: Kids?**  
  A: Yes.

# Hidden Leaders

> Chinese name: **王权暗战 Hidden Leaders** · Doc #111 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../111_%E7%8E%8B%E6%9D%83%E6%9A%97%E6%88%98_Hidden_Leaders.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/111)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–6 (best: 4-5) |
| Play time | 30–45 min |
| Age | 14+ |
| Complexity | ●●○○○ Light (2/5) |
| Tags | hidden teams, cards, tug-of-war, strategy |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** B4 / C3 / C4
- **Photos:** [155056](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155056.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 第三轮照片 155056：C3 / C4 层、B4 左下角、右侧 → 王权暗战 Hidden Leaders

## Overview

Two markers tug-of-war on a power track; each leader serves two factions; end conditions are checked in priority order.

## Setup

1. Deal each player a secret leader (two allegiances); markers start at 4; deal hero hands.

## How to play

1. Play a hero face up (use its ability) or face down (hide its faction).
2. Refill per rules.

## Game end & scoring

- Check in order: Undead (both in Dark War zone), Water Folk (adjacent/same), Empire (red ahead by 2+), Hill Clans (green ahead by 2+).

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Check in order: Undead (both in Dark War zone), Water Folk (adjacent/same), Empire (red ahead by 2+), Hill Clans (green ahead by 2+).
2. **What you do on a turn:** Play a hero face up (use its ability) or face down (hide its faction).
3. **Setup check:** Deal each player a secret leader (two allegiances); markers start at 4; deal hero hands.
4. **The one rule people forget:** The order of checks matters.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Pick the easier of your two paths.
- Read others' pushes.
- Face-down plays help in tiebreaks.

## Common mistakes

- The order of checks matters.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** most board gamers
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

> Written by the editor from the rules above as a walkthrough; numbers in the example are illustrative. The rulebook in the box always wins.

### Component checklist

From the Chinese doc (original wording):

- [ ] 游戏板（力量轨道，右端是“黑暗战争区”；板上还有港口、墓地、荒野、酒馆等区域）、红绿 2 个标记、**6 张领袖卡**、**77 张英雄卡**、6 张辅助卡。

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. Secret leaders; markers start at 4.
2. I play a hero face up: red advances 2.
3. He plays one face down.
4. Someone reaches the hero count — game ends.
5. Check order: not in dark war; markers adjacent — Water Folk win.

### Scoring example

- Faction checks in order; ties per rulebook.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- **Q: Why face down?**  
  A: Hides faction for tiebreaks.

### Player-count differences

- 2–6.
- Best with: 4-5 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- New edition.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 10 min (weight ●●).
- Play: 30–45 min; budget about 55 min for a first game.
- Total: about 65 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Two markers fight on a power track. Each leader secretly serves two factions; steer the track to favour yours. At the end victory is checked in order: Undead, Water Folk, Empire, Tribes.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Hide your factions.
- **Mid:** Read others' pushes.
- **Late:** Steer precisely at the end.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Two factions per player?**  
  A: Yes.
- **Q: Ties?**  
  A: See rulebook.

# Ghost Blitz: Spooky Doo

> Chinese name: **幽灵快手 鬼影幢幢** · Doc #112 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../112_%E5%B9%BD%E7%81%B5%E5%BF%AB%E6%89%8B%E9%AC%BC%E5%BD%B1%E5%B9%A2%E5%B9%A2_Ghost_Blitz_Spooky_Doo.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/112)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–8 (best: 3-6) |
| Play time | 20 min |
| Age | 8+ |
| Complexity | ●○○○○ Very light (1/5) |
| Tags | reaction, real-time, family, kids |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** B4 / C3 / C4
- **Photos:** [155056](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155056.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 第三轮照片 155056：C3 / C4 层、B4 左下角、右侧 → 幽灵快手：鬼影幢幢 Ghost Blitz: Spooky Doo（铁盒）

## Overview

Grab the right wooden object: if a card shows an object in its true colour, grab it; otherwise grab the one sharing neither colour nor shape.

## Setup

1. Place the 5 objects in the centre.

## How to play

1. Reveal a card; everyone grabs at once.
2. Rule 1: object in correct colour → grab it.
3. Rule 2: none correct → grab the object whose colour and shape are both absent.

## Game end & scoring

- Most cards wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Most cards wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Reveal a card; everyone grabs at once.
3. **Setup check:** Place the 5 objects in the centre.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Grab only one object.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Classify the card first.
- Wrong grabs give a card back.
- Add expansion rules later.

## Common mistakes

- Grab only one object.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** big parties; families with young children; short gaps — waiting for friends, before or after dinner; fans of speed and excitement
- **Not ideal for:** people who want to think slowly and quietly

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

> Written by the editor from the rules above as a walkthrough; numbers in the example are illustrative. The rulebook in the box always wins.

### Component checklist

From the Chinese doc (original wording):

- [ ] 铁盒、**5 个木制物件**（如白色幽灵、绿色瓶子、老鼠、**蓝色木桶**等，本作新增**木桶**；具体颜色以盒内为准）、**土耳其帽（Fez）**、图卡。

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. Flip a card.
2. Five items on the table.
3. An item matches in shape and colour — grab it.
4. If nothing matches, grab the item with neither shape nor colour shown.
5. Most cards wins.

### Scoring example

- Most cards.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- **Q: Wrong grab?**  
  A: Give back a card.

### Player-count differences

- 2–8.
- Best with: 3-6 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- Spooky Doo variant.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 5 min (weight ●).
- Play: 20–20 min; budget about 25 min for a first game.
- Total: about 30 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Flip a card and grab the right wooden object: if one object appears in its true colour, grab it; otherwise grab the one whose shape and colour are both absent. Correct grabs win the card; wrong grabs cost a card. Most cards wins.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Learn the five objects and colours.
- **Mid:** Use elimination fast.
- **Late:** Avoid false grabs.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Wrong grab?**  
  A: Give up a won card.
- **Q: What does the sequel add?**  
  A: New objects and rules.

|---|---|
| Players | 2–5 (best: 3-4) |
| Play time | 30–45 min |
| Age | 8+ |
| Complexity | ●●○○○ Light (2/5) |
| Tags | engine, resource conversion, hand management, family |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** D3 / E2 / E5 / F5
- **Photos:** [155109](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155109.jpg), [155128](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155128.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155109：E2–E5 层与 D3 层 → 香料世纪 Century 系列（香料之路、东方奇观 / 神秘东方、新世界）
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155128：F5 下方与左侧 → ★ 签签入扣 Stick Stack ×2、★ 我是大老板（左上）、★ 香料世纪（左侧 E 列）

## Overview

Spice Road: use merchant cards to produce, upgrade and trade four spices and claim point cards; the sequels stand alone or combine.

## Setup

1. Spices: yellow < red < green < brown; caravan limit 10.
2. 6 merchant cards and 5 point cards (gold and silver coins on the first two); starting hand of 2.

## How to play

1. Choose one: play a card, acquire a merchant (pay spices to skip cards), claim a point card, or rest (return played cards).

## Game end & scoring

- Game ends when someone claims their 5th (4–5p) or 6th point card; most points wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Game ends when someone claims their 5th (4–5p) or 6th point card; most points wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Choose one: play a card, acquire a merchant (pay spices to skip cards), claim a point card, or rest (return played cards).
3. **Setup check:** Spices: yellow < red < green < brown; caravan limit 10.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Discard spices over 10.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Build your engine early.
- Coins reward early claims.
- Time your rests.

## Common mistakes

- Discard spices over 10.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** most board gamers
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. 2 starting cards.
2. Play a produce card for 2 yellow.
3. Upgrade 2 yellow into red.
4. Claim a point card and a coin.
5. Game ends when someone claims 5 (4p).

### Scoring example

- Point cards + coins + non-yellow spices.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- **Q: How to recover cards?**  
  A: Rest action.

### Player-count differences

- 2–5.
- Best with: 3-4 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- Eastern Wonders, New World.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 10 min (weight ●●).
- Play: 30–45 min; budget about 55 min for a first game.
- Total: about 65 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Start with merchant cards. On your turn: play a card (produce, upgrade, trade spices); acquire a new card (leave spices on skipped ones); claim a point card; or rest to return played cards. The 5th (4–5p) or 6th (2–3p) point card triggers the end.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Get strong trade and upgrade cards early.
- **Mid:** Build a steady spice engine.
- **Late:** Grab point cards with coins late.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Spice limit?**  
  A: 10.
- **Q: Is resting an action?**  
  A: Yes.

# Stick Stack

> Chinese name: **签签入扣 Stick Stack** · Doc #117 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../117_%E7%AD%BE%E7%AD%BE%E5%85%A5%E6%89%A3_Stick_Stack.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/117)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–8 (best: 3-5) |
| Play time | 15 min |
| Age | 6+ |
| Complexity | ●○○○○ Very light (1/5) |
| Tags | dexterity, balance, stacking, family, kids |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** D3 / E2 / E5 / F5
- **Photos:** [155109](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155109.jpg), [155128](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155128.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155109：E2–E5 层与 D3 层 → 签签入扣 Stick Stack
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155128：F5 下方与左侧 → ★ 签签入扣 Stick Stack ×2、★ 我是大老板（左上）、★ 香料世纪（左侧 E 列）

## Overview

Dexterity balance game: draw a coloured stick and place it so every colour only touches the same colour on the tray or other sticks.

## Setup

1. Assemble base, pillar and tray; put the sticks in the bag.

## How to play

1. Draw 1 stick from the bag, or use one you collected from earlier falls.
2. Place it one-handed; at the moment it lands, each colour on the stick may only touch areas of the same colour.
3. Sticks that fall during your turn are yours.
4. Black edition: also follow a revealed instruction card (colour limit, which hand).

## Game end & scoring

- 2Plus quick rule: when the bag is empty, fewest sticks wins.
- English rules: round ends when all sticks are placed or the tower collapses; the toppler scores 5 points instead of collecting, others 1 point per stick; at 11+ the lowest score wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** 2Plus quick rule: when the bag is empty, fewest sticks wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Draw 1 stick from the bag, or use one you collected from earlier falls.
3. **Setup check:** Assemble base, pillar and tray; put the sticks in the bag.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Box player counts vary (2–8, 2+, 2–12).
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Place toward the centre.
- Counterbalance the tray.
- Move slowly.

## Common mistakes

- Box player counts vary (2–8, 2+, 2–12).

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** big parties; families with young children; short gaps — waiting for friends, before or after dinner; fans of speed and excitement
- **Not ideal for:** people who want to think slowly and quietly

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. Draw a stick.
2. Colours must touch matching colours.
3. Two sticks fall — they're mine.
4. I use a fallen stick next turn.
5. Bag empty: fewest sticks wins (2Plus).

### Scoring example

- Check your edition's end condition.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- See “Common mistakes” above; anything else — check the rulebook.

- 2+.
- Best with: 3-5 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- Black edition adds instruction cards.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 5 min (weight ●).
- Play: 15–15 min; budget about 20 min for a first game.
- Total: about 25 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Roll for a colour and hang sticks of that colour on the tray. The tray tilts; drop sticks and you take them. First to place all wins.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Hang near the balance point.
- **Mid:** Watch the tilt.
- **Late:** Steady hands late.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Dropped several?**  
  A: Take them all.
- **Q: Kids?**  
  A: Yes.

> Chinese name: **地外文明 SETI** · Doc #118 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../118_%E5%9C%B0%E5%A4%96%E6%96%87%E6%98%8E_SETI.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/118)

| Item | Value |

| Play time | 40–160 min |
| Age | 14+ |
| Complexity | ●●●●○ Medium-heavy (4/5) |
| Tags | heavy, sci-fi, card-driven, majority, resource management |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** none on the photo — inferred area: ≈ top of the E–G cabinets (above the E1/E2, F1/F2, G1/G2 signs) *(inferred)*

## Overview

Run a research institute: launch probes, scan stars, analyse data and find alien life on a rotating solar system.

## Setup

1. Each player: 4 publicity, 4 credits, 3 energy, 5 cards, income card.

## How to play

1. Main actions: launch, move, orbit or land, scan, analyse, play cards, etc.; free actions too.
2. The solar system rotates, changing distances.
3. Alien species are revealed as traces accumulate.

## Game end & scoring

- After the final round, score tiles, missions and species; most points wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** After the final round, score tiles, missions and species; most points wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Main actions: launch, move, orbit or land, scan, analyse, play cards, etc.; free actions too.
3. **Setup check:** Each player: 4 publicity, 4 credits, 3 energy, 5 cards, income card.
4. **The one rule people forget:** First games can exceed 2 hours.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Data and analysis lead to aliens.
- Time launches with rotation.
- Income choices shape the mid-game.

## Common mistakes

- First games can exceed 2 hours.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** experienced gamers who want a deep think
- **Not ideal for:** relaxed chatting sessions

## Overview

Cthulhu co-op adventure: the free app acts as keeper, generating maps, monsters and puzzles.

## Setup

1. Start the app, choose a scenario and investigators.

## How to play

1. Two actions per turn: move, search, interact, attack.
2. Horror checks reduce sanity; solve puzzles.
3. Mythos phase run by the app.

## Game end & scoring

- Scenario objectives revealed by the app.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Scenario objectives revealed by the app.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Two actions per turn: move, search, interact, attack.
3. **Setup check:** Start the app, choose a scenario and investigators.
4. **The one rule people forget:** The app is required.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Split up but avoid isolation.
- Sanity drains faster than health.
- Discuss clues together.

## Common mistakes

- The app is required.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** friends who prefer working together
- **Not ideal for:** when time is short

> Chinese name: **同房异梦 Decorum** · Doc #120 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../120_%E5%90%8C%E6%88%BF%E5%BC%82%E6%A2%A6_Decorum.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/120)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–4 (best: 2-3) |
| Play time | 30–45 min |
| Age | 13+ |
| Complexity | ●●●○○ Medium (3/5) |
| Tags | co-op, deduction, limited communication, hidden information |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** D1
- **Photos:** [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155121：中部与 D1 层 → 同房异梦 Décorum

## Overview

Housemates cooperate to decorate: each has secret conditions and can only react 'happy' or 'unhappy' to changes.

## Setup

1. Set up the house; choose a scenario; deal secret conditions.

## How to play

1. On your turn make one change (add, swap, remove).
2. Others react only with approval or disapproval.

## Game end & scoring

- Win if all conditions are satisfied within the limit.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Win if all conditions are satisfied within the limit.
2. **What you do on a turn:** On your turn make one change (add, swap, remove).
3. **Setup check:** Set up the house; choose a scenario; deal secret conditions.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Never state conditions.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Change one thing at a time.
- Log reactions.
- Compromise conflicting goals.

## Common mistakes

- Never state conditions.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** friends who prefer working together; people who love deduction and guessing identities
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

## Overview

Dungeon crawling plus drinking: the drink in your cup is your health; take damage, take a sip; empty cup and you are out.

## Setup

1. Each hero has a cup filled with a drink; build the dungeon start.

## How to play

1. Explore tiles, fight monsters and traps; damage means drinking.

## Game end & scoring

- Win per the rulebook (e.g. last standing or objective).

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Win per the rulebook (e.g. last standing or objective).
2. **What you do on a turn:** Explore tiles, fight monsters and traps; damage means drinking.
3. **Setup check:** Each hero has a cup filled with a drink; build the dungeon start.
4. **The one rule people forget:** 21+ in the original listing; drink responsibly.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Non-alcoholic drinks work too.
- It is about the party.
- Agree amounts first.

## Common mistakes

- 21+ in the original listing; drink responsibly.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** big parties; icebreakers; newcomers and grandparents can learn it instantly
- **Not ideal for:** minors

- Best with: 4-6 players.

- Expansions.

- **Mid:** Manage damage.
- **Late:** Beat the boss.

# Feed the Kraken

> Chinese name: **险恶迷航 Feed the Kraken** · Doc #122 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../122_%E9%99%A9%E6%81%B6%E8%BF%B7%E8%88%AA_Feed_the_Kraken.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/122)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 5–11 (best: 7-9) |
| Play time | 45–90 min |
| Age | 12+ |
| Complexity | ●●●○○ Medium (3/5) |
| Tags | hidden role, teams, deduction, three factions |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** G2 / G4
- **Photos:** [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155121：G2–G4 层 → 险恶迷航 Feed the Kraken

## Overview

Three factions on a pirate ship — sailors, pirates and cultists — want different destinations; elect officers, navigate and vote.

## Setup

1. Deal secret loyalties by player count.

## How to play

1. The captain nominates a lieutenant and navigator; others vote.
2. Navigation: officers secretly contribute direction cards.
3. Actions: mutiny, flogging, feeding someone to the kraken; the cult leader converts.

## Game end & scoring

- Ship reaches blue water (sailors), red (pirates), or the kraken (cultists).

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Ship reaches blue water (sailors), red (pirates), or the kraken (cultists).
2. **What you do on a turn:** The captain nominates a lieutenant and navigator; others vote.
3. **Setup check:** Deal secret loyalties by player count.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Different setups by player count.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Nominations are trust votes.
- Track navigation cards.
- Conversions can flip the game.

## Common mistakes

- Different setups by player count.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** big parties; people who love deduction and guessing identities
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

2. The captain nominates lieutenant and navigator; vote.
3. They pass navigation cards; the last card steers.
4. The ship drifts toward pirate waters.
5. Reaching a destination decides the winner.

### Scoring example

- The destination decides; the cult has its own condition.

  A: Yes, by mutiny per rulebook.

## Overview

Sci-fi conflict: trick-taking drives your actions; declare ambitions and fight over resources, court cards and territory.

## Setup

1. Deal action cards (Administration, Aggression, Construction, Mobilization).

## How to play

1. Lead a card; others surpass, copy or pivot.
2. Take actions per the card; declare ambitions.
3. Battle with dice; collect resources and court cards.

## Game end & scoring

- After three chapters, ambition scores decide the winner.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** After three chapters, ambition scores decide the winner.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Lead a card; others surpass, copy or pivot.
3. **Setup check:** Deal action cards (Administration, Aggression, Construction, Mobilization).
4. **The one rule people forget:** Many details — follow the rulebook.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Declare ambitions you can win.
- Pivot to grab actions the leader denies.
- Avoid attrition wars.

## Common mistakes

- Many details — follow the rulebook.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** experienced gamers who want a deep think
- **Not ideal for:** relaxed chatting sessions

## Overview

## Setup

1. Choose themed sets; each player has a starter deck.

## How to play

1. Draft cards, adjust your deck, then duel: flip cards comparing power; beaten cards go to the bench; a full bench loses.

## Game end & scoring

- After 7 rounds, top two play the final.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** After 7 rounds, top two play the final.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Draft cards, adjust your deck, then duel: flip cards comparing power; beaten cards go to the bench; a full bench loses.
3. **Setup check:** Choose themed sets; each player has a starter deck.
4. **The one rule people forget:** 45 minutes regardless of player count.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Theme synergies.
- Lean decks draw key cards.
- Watch bench limits.

## Common mistakes

- 45 minutes regardless of player count.

## Good for / not for

- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–4 (best: 3-4) |
| Play time | 45 min |
| Age | 10+ |

| Tags | bag-building, push-your-luck, deck-building, family |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** F2 / F3 / G3
- **Photos:** [155114](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155114.jpg), [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg), [155125](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155125.jpg)

- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155125：F3 / G3 层（与第四轮重叠） → ★ 江湖郎中、★ 猜心俱乐部、★ 七大奇迹、★ 季节传说、★ 寿司狗（Gamewright 红色竹帘盒）、★ 奶酪大盗 ×2+、★ 女巫镇 ×4、★ 无间风云 ×3、★ 脑洞量表（青绿色盒）

## Overview

Bag-building: draw ingredients into your pot; more than 7 in white cherry bombs explodes it; buy better ingredients.

## Setup

1. Each player has a pot, a bag of starting ingredients and a flask.

## How to play

1. All draw simultaneously, placing ingredients along the spiral.
2. White total over 7 explodes: choose points or buying.
3. Effects by colour book; rubies and fortune events.

## Game end & scoring

- After 9 rounds, most points wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** After 9 rounds, most points wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** All draw simultaneously, placing ingredients along the spiral.
3. **Setup check:** Each player has a pot, a bag of starting ingredients and a flask.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Exactly 7 white does not explode.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Buy efficient low-cost ingredients early.
- Compute explosion risk.
- Rat tails help trailing players.

## Common mistakes

- Exactly 7 white does not explode.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** most board gamers
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

# Mind Reading Club

> Chinese name: **猜心俱乐部** · Doc #126 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../126_%E7%8C%9C%E5%BF%83%E4%BF%B1%E4%B9%90%E9%83%A8.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/126)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 2–8 (best: 4-6) |
| Play time | 20–30 min |
| Age | 10+ |
| Complexity | ●○○○○ Very light (1/5) |
| Tags | party, mind-meld, betting, writing |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** F2 / F3 / G3
- **Photos:** [155114](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155114.jpg), [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg), [155125](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155125.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155114 / 155121：F2 / F3 行 → 猜心俱乐部
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155125：F3 / G3 层（与第四轮重叠） → ★ 江湖郎中、★ 猜心俱乐部、★ 七大奇迹、★ 季节传说、★ 寿司狗（Gamewright 红色竹帘盒）、★ 奶酪大盗 ×2+、★ 女巫镇 ×4、★ 无间风云 ×3、★ 脑洞量表（青绿色盒）

## Overview

A party game of writing matching answers: the asker poses a question with 10+ answers; everyone writes 6 different answers and flips them to find matches, or bets on how many matches occur.

## Setup

1. Each player: 4 pawns, 6 writing boards, a marker; 150 in chips; board and prize bowl in the centre with 30.
2. Set the round bonuses per the reference card.

## How to play

1. Ask: the asker poses a question with at least 10 answers (e.g. letters of the alphabet).
2. Answer: everyone, including the asker, writes 6 different answers secretly.
3. Act clockwise from the asker's left: reveal one of your answers — anyone with the same or very similar answer must reveal it, you win the whole bowl and the glass pawn advances per matching group; or pay 10 to predict where the glass pawn will finish (max 2 pawns per number).
4. A round ends when someone has revealed all 6 answers.

## Game end & scoring

- The asker checks unrevealed answers; each invalid answer costs 20.
- Pay out predictions; richest after the rounds wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** The asker checks unrevealed answers; each invalid answer costs 20.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Ask: the asker poses a question with at least 10 answers (e.g. letters of the alphabet).
3. **Setup check:** Each player: 4 pawns, 6 writing boards, a marker; 150 in chips; board and prize bowl in the centre with 30.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Questions need 10+ answers; no looking things up.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Common answers match more.
- Bet once you see how fast matches occur.
- Ask broad questions.

## Common mistakes

- Questions need 10+ answers; no looking things up.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** big parties; icebreakers; newcomers and grandparents can learn it instantly
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

- **Shelf code:** F2 / F3 / G3
- **Photos:** [155114](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155114.jpg), [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg), [155125](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155125.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155114 / 155121：F2 / F3 行 → 七大奇迹 7 Wonders（基础版）
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155125：F3 / G3 层（与第四轮重叠） → ★ 江湖郎中、★ 猜心俱乐部、★ 七大奇迹、★ 季节传说、★ 寿司狗（Gamewright 红色竹帘盒）、★ 奶酪大盗 ×2+、★ 女巫镇 ×4、★ 无间风云 ×3、★ 脑洞量表（青绿色盒）

## Overview

Draft cards over 3 ages: build resources, military, science, commerce and wonders; trade with neighbours.

## Setup

1. Each player takes a wonder board (A/B side) and 3 coins.

## How to play

1. Pick 1 card and pass the rest (left, right, left).
2. Build it, use it for a wonder stage, or discard for 3 coins.
3. Buy resources from neighbours for 2 coins each.

## Game end & scoring

- Military conflicts after each age; score 7 categories; most points wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Military conflicts after each age; score 7 categories; most points wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Pick 1 card and pass the rest (left, right, left).
3. **Setup check:** Each player takes a wonder board (A/B side) and 3 coins.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Chains build for free.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Hate-draft neighbours' needs.
- Commit to science or ignore it.
- Military only compares neighbours.

## Common mistakes

- Chains build for free.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** most board gamers
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

## Detailed supplement: components, worked example, scoring, FAQ, player counts, variants

> Written by the editor from the rules above as a walkthrough; numbers in the example are illustrative. The rulebook in the box always wins.

### Component checklist

- [ ] No complete public component list — use the list in the box.

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. 7 cards per age; pick 1, pass the rest.
2. I build a lumber yard for free.
3. I build military with wood.
4. Age end: military vs neighbours.
5. Score after 3 ages.

### Scoring example

- Military, coins, wonders, civic, science, commerce, guilds.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- **Q: Buy from neighbours?**  
  A: 2 coins each.

### Player-count differences

- 3–7.
- Best with: 4-5 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- Leaders, Cities etc.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 10 min (weight ●●).
- Play: 30–30 min; budget about 35 min for a first game.
- Total: about 45 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Three ages of simultaneous drafting: pick one, pass the rest. Build resource, military, science, commerce, civic, guild buildings and wonders. Buy resources from neighbours at 2 coins each. Score 7 categories.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Grab resources in Age I.
- **Mid:** Focus 1–2 paths.
- **Late:** Grab guilds and civics in Age III.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Buy neighbours' resources?**  
  A: 2 coins each.
- **Q: Science scoring?**  
  A: Squares per symbol + 7 per set.

## Overview

Draft sushi: pick one, pass the rest; score tempura, sashimi, dumplings, nigiri with wasabi; pudding at the end.

## Setup

## How to play

1. Pick a card simultaneously and pass the rest.
2. Chopsticks let you take two later.

## Game end & scoring

- Score each round; after round 3, puddings: most +6, fewest −6.

## 5-minute teaching order

2. **What you do on a turn:** Pick a card simultaneously and pass the rest.
3. **Setup check:** Deal hands by player count.
4. **The one rule people forget:** No pudding penalty in 2-player.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Count remaining sets.
- Wasabi + squid = 9.
- Do not ignore puddings.

## Common mistakes

- No pudding penalty in 2-player.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** icebreakers; newcomers and grandparents can learn it instantly; short gaps — waiting for friends, before or after dinner
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

# Cheese Thief

> Chinese name: **奶酪大盗** · Doc #129 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../129_%E5%A5%B6%E9%85%AA%E5%A4%A7%E7%9B%97_Cheese_Thief.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/129)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 4–8 (best: 5-6) |
| Play time | 10–15 min |
| Age | 8+ |
| Complexity | ●○○○○ Very light (1/5) |
| Tags | social deduction, dice, party, no elimination |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** F2 / F3 / G3
- **Photos:** [155114](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155114.jpg), [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg), [155125](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155125.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155114 / 155121：F2 / F3 行 → 奶酪大盗 Cheese Thief
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155125：F3 / G3 层（与第四轮重叠） → ★ 江湖郎中、★ 猜心俱乐部、★ 七大奇迹、★ 季节传说、★ 寿司狗（Gamewright 红色竹帘盒）、★ 奶酪大盗 ×2+、★ 女巫镇 ×4、★ 无间风云 ×3、★ 脑洞量表（青绿色盒）

## Overview

No-moderator 10-minute deduction: everyone secretly rolls a die; on the night count, players whose number is called wake; the thief steals the cheese.

## Setup

1. Roles by count: 4p 1 thief + 3 sleepyheads (2 dice each); 5p 1 thief + 4; 6p adds a scapegoat; 7–8p add more sleepyheads; accomplice limit 1–2.
2. Everyone secretly rolls in their cup; only you know your number.

## How to play

1. Night: everyone closes eyes; someone counts 1 to 6; players whose die shows that number open eyes.
2. A sleepyhead awake alone may peek at another die; the thief steals the cheese on his number and may recruit accomplices.
3. Day: discuss freely — lying is allowed, but you may not reveal cups or roles.
4. Vote simultaneously; the most-voted player reveals.

## Game end & scoring

- Catching the thief wins for the sleepyheads; otherwise the thief side wins (scapegoat rules per rulebook).

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Catching the thief wins for the sleepyheads; otherwise the thief side wins (scapegoat rules per rulebook).
2. **What you do on a turn:** Night: everyone closes eyes; someone counts 1 to 6; players whose die shows that number open eyes.
3. **Setup check:** Roles by count: 4p 1 thief + 3 sleepyheads (2 dice each); 5p 1 thief + 4; 6p adds a scapegoat; 7–8p add more sleepyheads; accomplice limit 1–2.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Never reveal your cup or role card.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Players awake together are each other's witnesses.
- The thief may lie about his number.
- Accomplices deflect.

## Common mistakes

- Never reveal your cup or role card.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** big parties; icebreakers; newcomers and grandparents can learn it instantly; short gaps — waiting for friends, before or after dinner
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. 6p: thief, 4 sleepyheads, 1 scapegoat; secret rolls.
2. Count 1 to 6 at night; matching players wake.
3. I wake alone on 3 and peek at a die: 5.
4. On 5 two players wake; the thief steals and the other sees him.
5. Discuss, vote together, and resolve per rulebook.

### Scoring example

- Team victory; nobody is eliminated.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- **Q: Can I peek when others are awake?**  
  A: No, only when alone.

### Player-count differences

- 4–8 (4p uses 2 dice each).
- Best with: 5-6 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- See rulebook.

## Advanced guide: teaching script, time budget, phase strategy, extra FAQ

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 5 min (weight ●).
- Play: 10–15 min; budget about 20 min for a first game.
- Total: about 25 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Mice roll to choose when they wake. The thief steals the cheese at their hour; a mouse awake alone may peek at others' dice. Vote at dawn. Catch the thief to win.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Remember what you saw.
- **Mid:** Cross-check stories.
- **Late:** Coordinate the vote.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Can the thief lie?**  
  A: Yes.
- **Q: Accomplices?**  
  A: See rulebook.

# Salem 1692

> Chinese name: **女巫镇 Salem 1692** · Doc #130 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../130_%E5%A5%B3%E5%B7%AB%E9%95%87_Salem_1692.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/130)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 4–12 (best: 6-10) |
| Play time | 30–60 min |
| Age | 10+ |
| Complexity | ●●○○○ Light (2/5) |
| Tags | social deduction, cards, teams, accusation |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** F2 / F3 / G3
- **Photos:** [155114](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155114.jpg), [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg), [155125](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155125.jpg), [204000](../../photos/web/IMG20261127204000.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155114 / 155121：F2 / F3 行 → 女巫镇 Salem 1692
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155125：F3 / G3 层（与第四轮重叠） → ★ 江湖郎中、★ 猜心俱乐部、★ 七大奇迹、★ 季节传说、★ 寿司狗（Gamewright 红色竹帘盒）、★ 奶酪大盗 ×2+、★ 女巫镇 ×4、★ 无间风云 ×3、★ 脑洞量表（青绿色盒）
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 204000：上层中部（铁网后货架） → 中间 CLASSICS 女巫镇

## Overview

Witch trials: accusations force trial cards to be revealed; witches convert at night; townsfolk must find all witches.

## Setup

1. Deal tryal cards (witch, constable, not a witch) and public character cards.

## How to play

1. Draw 2 or play cards (accusations, evidence, blue/green/red cards).
2. Seven accusation points force a tryal reveal.
3. Night: witches kill; constable protects.

## Game end & scoring

- Players with all tryals revealed die; teams win per rulebook.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Players with all tryals revealed die; teams win per rulebook.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Draw 2 or play cards (accusations, evidence, blue/green/red cards).
3. **Setup check:** Deal tryal cards (witch, constable, not a witch) and public character cards.
4. **The one rule people forget:** All tryal cards revealed = death.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Accuse suspects to force reveals.
- The constable protects at night.
- Find the original witch early.

## Common mistakes

- All tryal cards revealed = death.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** big parties; people who love deduction and guessing identities
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

# Good Cop Bad Cop

> Chinese name: **无间风云 Good Cop Bad Cop** · Doc #131 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../131_%E6%97%A0%E9%97%B4%E9%A3%8E%E4%BA%91_Good_Cop_Bad_Cop.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/131)

## At a glance

| Item | Value |
|---|---|
| Players | 4–8 (best: 6) |
| Play time | 10–20 min |
| Age | 10+ |
| Complexity | ●●○○○ Light (2/5) |
| Tags | hidden teams, deduction, shootout |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** F2 / F3 / G3
- **Photos:** [155114](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155114.jpg), [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg), [155125](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155125.jpg)
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155114 / 155121：F2 / F3 行 → 无间风云 Good Cop Bad Cop
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155125：F3 / G3 层（与第四轮重叠） → ★ 江湖郎中、★ 猜心俱乐部、★ 七大奇迹、★ 季节传说、★ 寿司狗（Gamewright 红色竹帘盒）、★ 奶酪大盗 ×2+、★ 女巫镇 ×4、★ 无间风云 ×3、★ 脑洞量表（青绿色盒）

## Overview

Hidden-faction shootout: three integrity cards decide whether you are honest or crooked; investigate, arm and shoot the opposing leader (Agent or Kingpin).

## Setup

1. Remove guns and cards whose top-right number exceeds the player count.
2. Shuffle the Agent and Kingpin with some integrity cards and deal 1 each, then deal 2 more; arrange face down — order never changes.
3. Deal 1 equipment card each; guns in the centre.

## How to play

1. Honest: majority honest and no Kingpin, or holding the Agent. Crooked: majority crooked and no Agent, or holding the Kingpin.
2. Choose one action: Investigate (peek at one face-down integrity card of anyone); Equip (draw equipment, then reveal one of yours); Arm (take a gun, then reveal one of yours); Shoot (shoot your target, return the gun).
3. Shot players reveal all integrity cards; normal cops die; the Agent or Kingpin takes a wound and draws equipment on the first hit and dies on the second.
4. Equipment is usable only when you hold exactly 1; it may be played after an action is announced (steal guns, disarm, swap cards, revive…).

## Game end & scoring

- Kingpin killed: honest side wins. Agent killed: crooked side wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** Kingpin killed: honest side wins. Agent killed: crooked side wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Honest: majority honest and no Kingpin, or holding the Agent. Crooked: majority crooked and no Agent, or holding the Kingpin.
3. **Setup check:** Remove guns and cards whose top-right number exceeds the player count.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Integrity card order is fixed.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Cross-check investigations.
- Aiming reveals intentions.
- Leaders survive the first shot.

## Common mistakes

- Integrity card order is fixed.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** big parties; people who love deduction and guessing identities; short gaps — waiting for friends, before or after dinner
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

Before you start:
- Count the components against the rulebook list; sort cards by type — a missing card can break the balance.
- Check tokens, dice and score markers; coins or generic tokens can stand in for missing pieces.
- Hand out reference cards before the first game to save rulebook time.

### Worked example (illustrative)

1. 3 integrity cards each.
2. I investigate A.
3. I take equipment and a gun.
4. I shoot A.
5. Kill the Agent or Kingpin.

### Scoring example

- Kill the enemy leader.

### Rules FAQ / rulings

- See “Common mistakes” above; anything else — check the rulebook.

### Player-count differences

- 4–8.
- Best with: 6 players.

### Official variants & expansions

- Bomb expansion.

> Summarised by the editor from the rules above for teaching and improving; the rulebook wins on conflicts.

### First-game time budget

- Teaching: about 10 min (weight ●●).
- Play: 10–20 min; budget about 25 min for a first game.
- Total: about 35 min.

### Teaching script (read it aloud)

> Undercover cops: each has 3 integrity cards, honest or crooked. Investigate, equip, aim or shoot. Take down the enemy leader (Agent or Kingpin) to win.

### Strategy by phase

- **Early:** Investigate neighbours.
- **Mid:** Protect your leader.
- **Late:** Shoot decisively.

### Extra FAQ

- **Q: Shoot the wrong person?**  
  A: They're wounded or out.
- **Q: Equipment?**  
  A: Special abilities.

> Chinese name: **季节传说（四季物语 Seasons）** · Doc #132 · [中文原文 / Chinese doc](../132_%E5%AD%A3%E8%8A%82%E4%BC%A0%E8%AF%B4_%E5%9B%9B%E5%AD%A3%E7%89%A9%E8%AF%AD_Seasons.md) · [Open in the web app](https://codinggeoff.github.io/boardgame-shelf-rules/#doc/132)

## At a glance

| Players | 2–4 (best: 2-3) |
| Play time | 60 min |
| Age | 14+ |
| Complexity | ●●●○○ Medium (3/5) |
| Tags | drafting, dice, engine, cards, strategy |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** F3 / G2 / G3 / G4
- **Photos:** [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg), [155125](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155125.jpg)

## Overview

Three years of 12 seasons: draft ability cards and split them across years, then pick seasonal dice to summon items and familiars for crystals.

## Setup

1. Prelude: draft 9 cards and split into 3 years of 3.

## How to play

1. Each season roll dice; players pick one (energy, crystals, summon slots, draw).
2. Summon cards with energy; transmute energy to crystals (rate depends on season).

## Game end & scoring

- After 3 years, score crystals and card points; bonus cards cost points.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** After 3 years, score crystals and card points; bonus cards cost points.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Each season roll dice; players pick one (energy, crystals, summon slots, draw).
3. **Setup check:** Prelude: draft 9 cards and split into 3 years of 3.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Likely a bootleg edition; component quality varies.
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Plan combos across years.
- Raise summon slots.
- Watch time-moving dice.

## Common mistakes

- Likely a bootleg edition; component quality varies.

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** most board gamers
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

- **Mid:** Exploit season changes.
- **Late:** Push crystals in year 3.

| Tags | betting, push-your-luck, family, party, dice |

## Where is it on the shelf?

- **Shelf code:** A3 / F2 / F3 / F4 / F5 / G4
- **Photos:** [155114](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155114.jpg), [155121](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155121.jpg), [155125](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155125.jpg), [155128](../../photos/web/IMG20261127155128.jpg)

- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155125 / 155128：F4 / G4 层 → ★ 骆驼快跑 ×2、★ 四海好伙伴、★ 玩具大战（贴 A3 标签）、★ 犯罪现场（G4，第 2 盒）
- Original note (Chinese): 照片 155128：F5 层透明收纳箱（蓝框编号标签） → 64 骆驼快跑

## Overview

Bet on a camel race where camels stack and carry those on top; bet on legs and overall winner and loser.

## Setup

1. Place camels; leg betting tiles; pyramid of dice.

## How to play

1. Choose one: take a leg bet, place a spectator tile, roll a pyramid die, bet on overall winner/loser.

## Game end & scoring

- When a camel crosses the finish, settle overall bets; most money wins.

## 5-minute teaching order

1. **Goal first:** When a camel crosses the finish, settle overall bets; most money wins.
2. **What you do on a turn:** Choose one: take a leg bet, place a spectator tile, roll a pyramid die, bet on overall winner/loser.
3. **Setup check:** Place camels; leg betting tiles; pyramid of dice.
4. **The one rule people forget:** Crazy camels move backwards (2nd ed.).
5. Play one open-handed practice round, then start for real.

## Strategy tips

- Top of the stack has the edge.
- Bet early on legs.
- Rolling earns 1 coin.

## Common mistakes

- Crazy camels move backwards (2nd ed.).

## Good for / not for

- **Good for:** big parties; icebreakers; newcomers and grandparents can learn it instantly
- **Not ideal for:** no obvious bad fit

- **Mid:** Use stacking to predict.
- **Late:** Bet on the overall winner late.
